We propose a novel test-time framework we dub probabilistic programs of thought to obtain more samples from the model with fewer LLM generations. Given a program generated by a model and the associated next-token probabilities, we build a probabilistic program that compactly represents exponentially many deterministic programs. Since performing probabilistic reasoning in this probabilistic program is much cheaper, our approach allows sampling new programs without any additional GPU compute and little CPU overhead. We instantiate our approach on benchmarks for code generation, code understanding and mathematical reasoning and report improvements in performance with fewer generations from the LLM.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond "I Don't Know": Evaluating LLM Self-Awareness in Discriminating Data and Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17293)
*Jingyi Ren,Ante Wang,Yunghwei Lai,Xiaolong Wang,Linlu Gong,Weitao Li,Weizhi Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出UA-Bench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型区分数据不确定性和模型不确定性的能力，并开发了轻量级数据合成和强化学习策略来改善这种区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将大语言模型的拒绝回答视为通用的"我不知道"，未能区分输入层面的模糊性（数据不确定性）和能力限制（模型不确定性）。这种缺乏区分限制了后续行动决策，如请求澄清或调用外部工具。

Method: 1. 引入UA-Bench基准测试，包含来自六个数据集的3500多个问题，涵盖知识密集型和推理密集型任务；2. 提出轻量级数据合成和强化学习策略来改善不确定性归因能力；3. 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和Qwen3-8B模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 评估18个前沿大语言模型发现，即使最先进的模型也难以可靠地区分数据不确定性和模型不确定性，高答案准确率并不一定意味着强的不确定性归因能力。提出的方法在保持答案准确率的同时，改善了不确定性归因能力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型需要更好地区分不同类型的不确定性，提出的基准测试和方法为改善不确定性归因能力提供了有效途径，有助于实现更可靠的拒绝回答机制。

Abstract: Reliable Large Language Models (LLMs) should abstain when confidence is insufficient. However, prior studies often treat refusal as a generic "I don't know'', failing to distinguish input-level ambiguity (data uncertainty) from capability limitations (model uncertainty). This lack of distinction limits downstream action decisions like requesting clarification or invoking external tools. In this work, we introduce UA-Bench, a benchmark of over 3,500 questions drawn from six datasets spanning knowledge-intensive and reasoning-intensive tasks, designed to evaluate explicit uncertainty attribution. An evaluation of 18 frontier LLMs shows that even state-of-the-art models struggle to reliably discriminate between data uncertainty and model uncertainty, and that high answer accuracy does not necessarily imply strong uncertainty attribution ability. To narrow this gap, we propose a lightweight data synthesis and reinforcement learning strategy. Experiments on both Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-8B in thinking mode show that the proposed method improves uncertainty attribution while preserving answer accuracy. Our code and data are publicly available now.

</details>


### [152] [CRISP: Compressing Redundancy in Chain-of-Thought via Intrinsic Saliency Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17297)
*Yangsong Lan,Hongliang Dai,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRISP框架通过利用模型内在显著性压缩思维链推理，减少50-60%的token数量而不损失准确性，解决长推理链的计算开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长思维链推理虽然提升了推理模型的性能，但存在高计算开销和延迟问题。现有压缩方法使用外部压缩器，往往无法与模型内部推理动态对齐，导致关键逻辑步骤丢失。

Method: 提出CRISP框架，通过分析发现推理终止token作为信息锚点，其注意力模式能有效区分必要推理和冗余信息。基于这一发现，设计利用内在注意力信号指导原子压缩操作的策略，相比粗粒度剪枝方法，CRISP策略性地蒸馏推理链以最大化信息密度同时保持逻辑连贯性。

Result: 在多种骨干模型和数学数据集上的实验结果表明，CRISP实现了50-60%的token数量减少，且不损害准确性，有效缓解了长上下文推理的效率瓶颈。

Conclusion: CRISP通过利用模型内在显著性压缩思维链推理，提供了一种高效解决长推理链计算开销的方法，开源实现将促进高效推理研究的进一步发展。

Abstract: Long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning is pivotal for the success of recent reasoning models but suffers from high computational overhead and latency. While prior works attempt to compress CoT via external compressor, they often fail to align with the model's internal reasoning dynamics, resulting in the loss of critical logical steps. This paper presents \textbf{C}ompressing \textbf{R}edundancy in Chain-of-Thought via \textbf{I}ntrinsic \textbf{S}aliency \textbf{P}runing (\textbf{CRISP}), a framework that compresses CoT by exploiting the model's intrinsic saliency. Our analysis reveals a distinct phenomenon: the reasoning termination token \texttt{[object Object]} acts as an information anchor, where its attention pattern effectively demarcates essential reasoning from redundancy. Based on this finding, we design a policy that utilizes these intrinsic attention signals to guide atomic compression operations. In contrast to coarse-grained pruning strategies, CRISP strategically distills the reasoning chain to maximize information density while preserving logical coherence. Empirical results across various backbone models and mathematical datasets demonstrate that CRISP achieves a 50-60% reduction in token count without compromising accuracy, effectively mitigating the efficiency bottleneck of long-context reasoning. We open-source our implementation to facilitate further research in efficient reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [RoTRAG: Rule of Thumb Reasoning for Conversation Harm Detection with Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17301)
*Juhyeon Lee,Wonduk Seo,Junseo Koh,Seunghyun Lee,Haihua Chen,Yi Bu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoTRAG：一种检索增强框架，通过引入人类编写的道德规范（经验法则）来改进多轮对话中的有害内容检测，提高分类准确性、可解释性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖模型内部参数知识，缺乏外部规范性原则的明确基础，导致在社交细微语境中判断不一致、可解释性有限，以及跨轮次冗余推理。

Method: 提出RoTRAG框架，从外部语料库检索相关经验法则作为规范性证据，用于轮次级推理和最终严重性分类。引入轻量级二元路由分类器决定新轮次是否需要检索增强推理或可重用现有上下文。

Result: 在ProsocialDialog和Safety Reasoning Multi Turn Dialogue数据集上，RoTRAG相比基线方法在有害分类和严重性估计方面均有提升，F1分数平均相对增益约40%，分布误差平均相对减少8.4%，同时减少冗余计算而不牺牲性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式整合人类编写的道德规范，RoTRAG能够提供更一致、可解释且高效的多轮对话有害内容检测，解决了现有方法在规范性基础和计算效率方面的局限性。

Abstract: Detecting harmful content in multi turn dialogue requires reasoning over the full conversational context rather than isolated utterances. However, most existing methods rely mainly on models internal parametric knowledge, without explicit grounding in external normative principles. This often leads to inconsistent judgments in socially nuanced contexts, limited interpretability, and redundant reasoning across turns. To address this, we propose RoTRAG, a retrieval augmented framework that incorporates concise human written moral norms, called Rules of Thumb (RoTs), into LLM based harm assessment. For each turn, RoTRAG retrieves relevant RoTs from an external corpus and uses them as explicit normative evidence for turn level reasoning and final severity classification. To improve efficiency, we further introduce a lightweight binary routing classifier that decides whether a new turn requires retrieval grounded reasoning or can reuse existing context. Experiments on ProsocialDialog and Safety Reasoning Multi Turn Dialogue show that RoTRAG consistently improves both harm classification and severity estimation over competitive baselines, with an average relative gain of around 40% in F1 across benchmark datasets and an average relative reduction of 8.4% in distributional error, while reducing redundant computation without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [154] [Calibrated? Not for Everyone: How Sexual Orientation and Religious Markers Distort LLM Accuracy and Confidence in Medical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17316)
*Alberto Testoni,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现患者的社会身份描述符（性取向和宗教信仰）会扭曲大型语言模型的不确定性校准信号和准确性，导致"校准危机"，对临床安全部署构成风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确保大型语言模型在临床环境中的安全部署，不仅需要高准确性，还需要稳健的不确定性校准，使模型在适当时能够将决策权交给临床医生。本研究旨在探究患者的社会身份描述如何影响这些不确定性信号和模型准确性。

Method: 评估了9个通用和生物医学大型语言模型，使用2,364个医学问题及其反事实变体。通过对比包含不同社会身份标记（性取向和宗教信仰）的问题，分析模型性能变化。同时进行了临床医生验证的案例研究，在开放式生成环境中确认发现。

Result: 身份标记导致"校准危机"："同性恋"标记持续引发性能下降，交叉身份产生特异性的非叠加性校准损害。这些失败并非多项选择题格式的假象，临床验证案例研究证实了相同问题。社会身份线索不仅改变预测结果，还影响置信度信号的可靠性。

Conclusion: 患者社会身份描述符会扭曲大型语言模型的不确定性校准，对基于置信度的临床工作流程中的公平护理和安全部署构成重大风险。这强调了在临床环境中部署AI时需要解决社会偏见对模型置信度评估的影响。

Abstract: Safe clinical deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) requires not only high accuracy but also robust uncertainty calibration to ensure models defer to clinicians when appropriate. Our paper investigates how social descriptors of a patient (specifically sexual orientation and religious affiliation) distort these uncertainty signals and model accuracy. Evaluating nine general-purpose and biomedical LLMs on 2,364 medical questions and their counterfactual variants, we demonstrate that identity markers cause a "calibration crisis". "Homosexual" markers consistently trigger performance drops, and intersectional identities produce idiosyncratic, non-additive harms to calibration. Moreover, a clinician-validated case study in an open-ended generation setting confirms that these failures are not an artifact of the multiple-choice format. Our results demonstrate that the presence of social identity cues does not merely shift predictions; it affects the reliability of confidence signals, posing a significant risk to equitable care and safe deployment in confidence-based clinical workflows.

</details>


### [155] [A Universal Avoidance Method for Diverse Multi-branch Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17323)
*Kyeongman Park,Minha Jhang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UAG是一种模型无关、计算高效的生成策略，通过惩罚先前生成输出的相似性来增强多分支多样性，适用于扩散和Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型在创造力方面仍缺乏人类水平，特别是在多分支多样性方面。现有方法通常计算成本高或对模型架构依赖性强。

Method: 提出UAG（通用避免生成）策略，这是一种模型无关且计算高效的生成方法，通过惩罚已生成输出之间的相似性来增强多样性。

Result: 实验表明，UAG相比最先进方法实现了高达1.9倍的多样性提升，运行速度快4.4倍，仅需1/64的FLOPs。

Conclusion: UAG是一种有效的模型无关方法，能够显著提升生成模型的多分支多样性，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: Modern generative models still lack human-level creativity, particularly in multi-branch diversity. Prior approaches to address this problem often incur heavy computation or strong dependency on model architecture. Therefore, we introduce UAG(Universal Avoidance Generation), a model-agnostic and computationally efficient generation strategy that penalizes similarity among previously generated outputs. Thus, UAG can enhance multi-branch diversity across both diffusion and transformer models, with minimal additional computation. In experiments, our method achieves up to 1.9 times higher diversity, runs 4.4 times faster, and requires only 1/64 of the FLOPs compared to state-of-the-art methods. The full code is https://anonymous.4open.science/r/2026_ACL_Universal/.

</details>


### [156] [Align Documents to Questions: Question-Oriented Document Rewriting for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17325)
*Jiaang Li,Zhendong Mao,Quan Wang,Yuning Wan,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QREAM是一种风格控制的重写器，通过将检索文档与问题导向风格对齐，解决RAG中LLM偏好流畅但幻觉生成内容而非事实性但混乱检索证据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在检索增强生成中，大语言模型存在风格偏见，倾向于选择流畅但可能产生幻觉的生成内容，而不是事实性强但组织混乱的检索证据，这限制了检索信息的效用。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) QREAM-ICL使用风格种子指导迭代重写探索；2) QREAM-FT是从去噪ICL输出中蒸馏出的轻量级学生模型，采用基于答案正确性和事实一致性的双重标准拒绝采样。

Result: 实验表明QREAM能持续提升先进RAG管道的性能，实现高达8%的相对改进，且延迟开销可忽略不计，有效平衡了问题相关性与事实基础。

Conclusion: QREAM作为即插即用模块，通过风格控制重写器将检索文档与问题导向风格对齐，同时保持事实性，帮助LLM读者更好地利用检索信息，解决了RAG中风格偏见导致的检索信息效用瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the factuality of Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating retrieved documents and/or generated context. However, LLMs often exhibit a stylistic bias when presented with mixed contexts, favoring fluent but hallucinated generated content over factually grounded yet disorganized retrieved evidence. This phenomenon reveals that the utility of retrieved information is bottlenecked by its presentation. To bridge this gap, we propose QREAM, a style-controlled rewriter that aligns retrieved documents with a question-oriented style while preserving facts, better for LLM readers to utilize. Our framework consists of two stages: (1) QREAM-ICL, which uses stylistic seeds to guide iterative rewriting exploration; and (2) QREAM-FT, a lightweight student model distilled from denoised ICL outputs. QREAM-FT employs dual-criteria rejection sampling, filtering based on answer correctness and factual consistency to ensure high-quality supervision. QREAM seamlessly integrates into existing RAG pipelines as a plug-and-play module. Experiments demonstrate that QREAM consistently enhances advanced RAG pipelines, yielding up to 8% relative improvement with negligible latency overhead, effectively balancing question relevance with factual grounding.

</details>


### [157] [Neuro-Symbolic Resolution of Recommendation Conflicts in Multimorbidity Clinical Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17340)
*Shiyao Xie,Jian Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出神经符号框架，通过多智能体系统将临床指南转换为符号逻辑，使用SAT求解器检测推荐冗余和冲突，特别关注多病共存导致的局部冲突，在SGLT2抑制剂指南评估中取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床指南由不同专业学会独立制定，存在碎片化、冗余和逻辑矛盾，特别是应用于多病患者时，不仅导致临床医生认知失调，还会给AI系统引入灾难性噪声，使标准RAG系统脆弱且易产生幻觉。

Method: 引入神经符号框架，使用多智能体系统将非结构化临床自然语言转换为严格的符号逻辑语言，通过SAT求解器验证，制定逻辑规则交互的层次分类法，特别识别"局部冲突"这一关键类别。

Result: 在12个权威SGLT2抑制剂指南的基准测试中，发现90.6%的冲突是局部冲突，这是单病指南无法解决的结构复杂性。神经符号方法F1分数达到0.861，而最先进的LLM无法检测这些冲突。

Conclusion: 逻辑验证必须先于检索，为医学AI中的自动化知识协调建立了新的技术标准。神经符号方法能有效解决临床指南中的逻辑不一致问题，特别是多病共存导致的冲突。

Abstract: Clinical guidelines, typically developed by independent specialty societies, inherently exhibit substantial fragmentation, redundancy, and logical contradiction. These inconsistencies, particularly when applied to patients with multimorbidity, not only cause cognitive dissonance for clinicians but also introduce catastrophic noise into AI systems, rendering the standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system fragile and prone to hallucination. To address this fundamental reliability crisis, we introduce a Neuro-Symbolic framework that automates the detection of recommendation redundancies and conflicts. Our pipeline employs a multi-agent system to translate unstructured clinical natural language into rigorous symbolic logic language, which is then verified by a Satisfiability (SAT) solver. By formulating a hierarchical taxonomy of logical rule interactions, we identify a critical category termed Local Conflict - a decision conflict arising from the intersection of comorbidities. Evaluating our system on a curated benchmark of 12 authoritative SGLT2 inhibitor guidelines, we reveal that 90.6% of conflicts are Local, a structural complexity that single-disease guidelines fail to address. While state-of-the-art LLMs fail in detecting these conflicts, our neuro-symbolic approach achieves an F1 score of 0.861. This work demonstrates that logical verification must precede retrieval, establishing a new technical standard for automated knowledge coordination in medical AI.

</details>


### [158] [Logical Computational Linguistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17346)
*Glyn V. Morrill,Oriol Valentín*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本书倡导逻辑计算语言学而非统计计算语言学，提出逻辑语义接口，汇集20多年类型逻辑语法研究成果并加入新思想


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为统计计算语言学存在置信度衰减问题，而逻辑依赖能保持100%置信度，旨在为生命关键NLP应用提供完美的句法和语义处理

Method: 基于类型逻辑语法构建逻辑语义接口，整合20多年研究成果并加入新思想，强调逻辑依赖的可靠性

Result: 建立了逻辑计算语言学的理论框架，展示了逻辑依赖在保持置信度方面的优势，为高可靠性NLP应用提供基础

Conclusion: 逻辑计算语言学比统计方法更适合生命关键应用，逻辑依赖能确保100%置信度，类型逻辑语法是实现完美NLP处理的有效途径

Abstract: In this book we promote logical computational linguistics as opposed to statistical computational linguistics. In particular, we provide a logical semantic interface. This book assembles more than twenty years of research work on type logical grammar, and adds new ideas and material.
  Chains of statistical dependencies of less than one hundred per cent confidence tend monotonically to zero. Chains of logical dependencies of any length maintain one hundred per cent confidence end to end.
  We aspire to enable perfect syntactic and semantic processing in life-critical NLP applications.

</details>


### [159] [More Than Meets the Eye: Measuring the Semiotic Gap in Vision-Language Models via Semantic Anchorage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17354)
*Wei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DIVA基准测试揭示视觉语言模型存在字面意义偏好偏差，视觉保真度越高，符号对齐越弱，表明超现实图像会干扰抽象意义的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉语言模型在表示抽象意义（如名词短语的习语解释）方面的局限性，探索高视觉保真度是否干扰视觉抽象下的习语组合性。

Method: 引入DIVA基准测试，用示意性图标代替高保真视觉细节，为字面和习语解读生成配对、意义锚定的可视化；提出语义对齐差距(Δ)和方向性符号偏差b(t)两个架构无关的度量指标。

Result: 评估8个最新视觉语言模型发现一致的字面意义优越性偏差：模型规模本身不能解决字面偏好，增加的视觉保真度与较弱的符号对齐相关，表明超现实图像会产生认知干扰。

Conclusion: 改进组合理解需要视觉输入的图标抽象，并将解释和生成锚定在意向意义上。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at photorealistic generation, yet often struggle to represent abstract meaning such as idiomatic interpretations of noun compounds. To study whether high visual fidelity interferes with idiomatic compositionality under visual abstraction, we introduce DIVA, a controlled benchmark that replaces high-fidelity visual detail with schematic iconicity by generating paired, sense-anchored visualizations for literal and idiomatic readings. We further propose Semantic Alignment Gap ($Δ$), an architecture-agnostic metric that quantifies divergence between literal and idiomatic visual grounding. We additionally introduce a directional signed bias $b(t)$ to separately measure the direction and strength of literal preference. Evaluating 8 recent VLMs, we reveal a consistent Literal Superiority Bias: model scale alone does not resolve literal preference, and increased visual fidelity is associated with weaker symbolic alignment, suggesting cognitive interference from hyper-realistic imagery. Our findings suggest that improving compositional understanding requires iconographic abstraction of visual input and anchoring interpretation and generation in intended meaning.

</details>


### [160] [Still Between Us? Evaluating and Improving Voice Assistant Robustness to Third-Party Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17358)
*Dongwook Lee,Eunwoo Song,Che Hyun Lee,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对口语语言模型无法区分第三方干扰的问题，提出了TPI-Train数据集和TPI-Bench评估框架，通过强调声学线索来提升模型在多说话人场景中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的口语语言模型在真实场景中部署时，缺乏区分第三方干扰与主要用户持续对话的能力，容易导致上下文错误。现有模型过度依赖语义信息而忽视声学线索，无法准确识别说话人切换。

Method: 研究团队创建了TPI-Train数据集（包含88K个样本），设计了说话人感知的困难负样本以强制模型优先考虑声学线索。同时开发了TPI-Bench评估框架，用于系统评估模型的干扰处理策略和在欺骗性语境中的说话人辨别能力。

Result: 实验表明，该数据集设计有效缓解了语义捷径学习问题（即模型过度依赖语义上下文而忽视识别说话人变化所需的声学信号）。提出的方法为口语语言模型克服文本主导的单模态依赖奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 这项工作为解决口语语言模型在多说话人交互中的鲁棒性问题提供了基础资源，为更强大的多方口语交互系统铺平了道路。相关代码已公开。

Abstract: While recent Spoken Language Models (SLMs) have been actively deployed in real-world scenarios, they lack the capability to discern Third-Party Interruptions (TPI) from the primary user's ongoing flow, leaving them vulnerable to contextual failures. To bridge this gap, we introduce TPI-Train, a dataset of 88K instances designed with speaker-aware hard negatives to enforce acoustic cue prioritization for interruption handling, and TPI-Bench, a comprehensive evaluation framework designed to rigorously measure the interruption-handling strategy and precise speaker discrimination in deceptive contexts. Experiments demonstrate that our dataset design mitigates semantic shortcut learning-a critical pitfall where models exploit semantic context while neglecting acoustic signals essential for discerning speaker changes. We believe our work establishes a foundational resource for overcoming text-dominated unimodal reliance in SLMs, paving the way for more robust multi-party spoken interaction. The code for the framework is publicly available at https://tpi-va.github.io

</details>


### [161] [ArgBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Computational Argumentation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17366)
*Yamen Ajjour,Carlotta Quensel,Nedim Lipka,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个用于评估大语言模型在计算论证任务上的标准化基准，包含33个数据集和46个任务，评估了5个LLM家族在不同论证技能上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证技能对大语言模型至关重要，可用于自我反思、协作辩论、反驳仇恨言论等场景。目前缺乏标准化的评估基准来系统评估LLM在计算论证任务上的能力。

Method: 创建了首个计算论证基准，整合了33个现有数据集，统一格式后形成46个任务，涵盖论证挖掘、视角评估、论证质量评估、论证推理和论证生成五个方面。评估了5个LLM家族，分析了few-shot示例、推理步骤、模型规模和训练技能对性能的影响。

Result: 通过基准测试系统评估了不同LLM在计算论证任务上的表现，发现few-shot示例、推理步骤、模型规模和特定训练技能都对性能有显著影响，为LLM在论证任务上的能力提供了全面评估。

Conclusion: 该基准为标准化评估LLM在计算论证任务上的能力提供了重要工具，揭示了影响LLM论证性能的关键因素，为未来LLM在论证相关应用的发展提供了指导。

Abstract: Argumentation skills are an essential toolkit for large language models (LLMs). These skills are crucial in various use cases, including self-reflection, debating collaboratively for diverse answers, and countering hate speech. In this paper, we create the first benchmark for a standardized evaluation of LLM-based approaches to computational argumentation, encompassing 33 datasets from previous work in unified form. Using the benchmark, we evaluate the generalizability of five LLM families across 46 computational argumentation tasks that cover mining arguments, assessing perspectives, assessing argument quality, reasoning about arguments, and generating arguments. On the benchmark, we conduct an extensive systematic analysis of the contribution of few-shot examples, reasoning steps, model size, and training skills to the performance of LLMs on the computational argumentation tasks in the benchmark.

</details>


### [162] [AnchorMem: Anchored Facts with Associative Contexts for Building Memory in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17377)
*Zhanyu Shen,Sijie Cheng,Zhicheng Guo,Weiqin Wang,Yile Wang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AnchorMem是一个新颖的记忆框架，通过解耦检索单元与生成上下文，使用原子事实作为检索锚点，同时保留原始上下文作为不可变背景，以解决现有记忆方法过度依赖总结而丢失关键上下文细节的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆方法（如A-Mem、Mem0）过度强调通过频繁重写来组织交互，这种对总结的严重依赖会稀释必要的上下文细微差别并模糊关键检索特征。需要一种能够平衡细粒度检索与上下文完整性的记忆系统。

Method: AnchorMem受认知科学中普鲁斯特现象的启发，提出解耦检索单元与生成上下文的方法：从交互历史中提取原子事实作为检索锚点，同时保留原始上下文作为不可变背景。构建关联事件图，使用高阶事件链接将相关事实绑定到共享事件表示中，增强跨记忆整合。检索时，系统将查询锚定到特定事实和事件来定位相关记忆，但使用关联的原始块和事件重建上下文。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准测试上，使用三个闭源和开源模型的实验表明，AnchorMem显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: AnchorMem通过解耦检索单元与生成上下文，实现了细粒度检索与交互上下文完整性的平衡，为长期交互中的记忆系统提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While large language models have achieved remarkable performance in complex tasks, they still need a memory system to utilize historical experience in long-term interactions. Existing memory methods (e.g., A-Mem, Mem0) place excessive emphasis on organizing interactions by frequently rewriting them, however, this heavy reliance on summarization risks diluting essential contextual nuances and obscuring key retrieval features. To bridge this gap, we introduce AnchorMem, a novel memory framework inspired by the Proust Phenomenon in cognitive science, where a specific anchor triggers a holistic recollection. We propose a method that decouples the retrieval unit from the generation context. AnchorMem extracts atomic facts from interaction history to serve as retrieval anchors, while preserving the original context as the immutable context. To reveal implicit narrative cues, we construct an associative event graph that uses higher-order event links that bind sets of related facts into shared event representations, strengthening cross-memory integration without relying on generic entities as bridges. During retrieval, the system anchors queries to specific facts and events to locate relevant memories, but reconstructs the context using the associated raw chunks and events. Our method reconciles fine-grained retrieval with the contextual integrity of interactions. Experiments across three closed-source and open-source models on the LoCoMo benchmark demonstrate that AnchorMem significantly outperforms baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/RayNeo-AI-2025/AnchorMem.

</details>


### [163] [Who Watches the Watchmen? Humans Disagree With Translation Metrics on Unseen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17393)
*Finn Schmidt,Jan Philip Wahle,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建跨领域错误标注数据集CD-ESA，发现自动评估指标在领域迁移中看似稳健，但考虑人类标注差异后稳健性显著下降，建议评估时应比较指标-人类一致性而非仅看原始一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器翻译自动评估指标大多基于WMT基准开发，其在未见领域的鲁棒性不明确。先前研究分析未见领域时存在翻译系统、标注者或评估条件变化的问题，混淆了领域效应与人类标注噪声。

Method: 引入系统性多标注者跨领域错误标注数据集CD-ESA，包含3个语言对的18.8k个人类错误标注，固定每个语言对的标注者，评估相同6个翻译系统在一个已见新闻领域和两个未见技术领域的翻译表现。

Result: 自动评估指标在片段级别看似对领域迁移具有鲁棒性（一致性高达0.69），但考虑人类标注差异后鲁棒性大幅下降。平均标注可将标注者间一致性提高+0.11。指标在未见化学领域表现较差（标注者间一致性0.78-0.83 vs. 0.96）。

Conclusion: 建议在不同领域评估时，应将指标-人类一致性与标注者间一致性进行比较，而非仅关注原始指标-人类一致性，以更准确评估指标性能。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are central to the development of machine translation systems, yet their robustness under domain shift remains unclear. Most metrics are developed on the Workshop on Machine Translation (WMT) benchmarks, raising concerns about their robustness to unseen domains. Prior studies that analyze unseen domains vary translation systems, annotators, or evaluation conditions, confounding domain effects with human annotation noise.
  To address these biases, we introduce a systematic multi-annotator Cross-Domain Error-Span-Annotation dataset (CD-ESA), comprising 18.8k human error span annotations across three language pairs, where we fix annotators within each language pair and evaluate translations of the same six translation systems across one seen news domain and two unseen technical domains. Using this dataset, we first find that automatic metrics appear surprisingly robust to domain-shifts at the segment level (up to 0.69 agreement), but this robustness largely disappears once we account for human label variation. Averaging annotations increases inter-annotator agreement by up to +0.11. Metrics struggle on the unseen chemical domain compared to humans (inter-annotator agreement of 0.78-0.83 vs. 0.96).
  We recommend comparing metric-human agreement against inter-annotator agreement, rather than comparing raw metric-human agreement alone, when evaluating across different domains.

</details>


### [164] [Representation-Guided Parameter-Efficient LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17396)
*Zeguan Xiao,Lang Mo,Yun Chen,Lei Yang,Jiehui Zhao,Lili Yang,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REGLU是一种基于表示空间几何特性的低秩遗忘学习方法，通过表示引导的LoRA初始化和正交约束正则化，在TOFU和WMDP基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常记忆敏感或有害信息，需要有效的机器遗忘技术。现有的参数高效遗忘方法虽然表现出潜力，但在遗忘-保留权衡方面仍有困难，这主要源于它们依赖参数重要性指标来识别仅与遗忘集相关的重要参数，而这种现象受到叠加现象的根本限制。由于LLM参数的多义性，这种重要性指标难以区分与遗忘集和保留集相关的参数。

Method: 提出了表示引导的低秩遗忘学习（REGLU）方法：1）开发了表示引导的LoRA初始化，识别选择性遗忘的最优子空间；2）引入了正则化损失，约束LoRA更新的输出位于保留集表示子空间的正交补空间中，从而最小化对保留集性能的干扰。

Result: 在TOFU和WMDP基准测试中，REGLU在多个模型上始终优于最先进的基线方法，实现了更优的遗忘质量，同时保持了更高的模型效用。

Conclusion: REGLU通过利用表示空间的几何特性，提供了一种稳健且精确的遗忘学习方法，有效解决了现有方法在遗忘-保留权衡方面的局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often memorize sensitive or harmful information, necessitating effective machine unlearning techniques. While existing parameter-efficient unlearning methods have shown promise, they still struggle with the forget-retain trade-off. This can be attributed to their reliance on parameter importance metrics to identify parameters that are important exclusively for the forget set, which is fundamentally limited by the superposition phenomenon. Due to the polysemantic nature of LLM parameters, such an importance metric may struggle to disentangle parameters associated with the forget and retain sets. In this work, we propose Representation-Guided Low-rank Unlearning (REGLU), a novel approach that leverages the geometric properties of representation spaces to achieve robust and precise unlearning. First, we develop a representation-guided initialization for LoRA that identifies the optimal subspace for selective forgetting. Second, we introduce a regularization loss that constrains the outputs of the LoRA update to lie in the orthogonal complement of the retain set's representation subspace, thereby minimizing interference with the model's performance on the retain set. We evaluate REGLU on the TOFU and WMDP benchmarks across multiple models. Our results demonstrate that REGLU consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior unlearning quality while maintaining higher model utility.

</details>


### [165] [Contrastive Analysis of Linguistic Representations in Large Language Model Outputs through Structured Synthetic Data Generation and Abstracted N-gram Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17398)
*S. A. Desimone,L. Alonso Alemany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过对比合成文本生成和统计分析来发现不同社会群体相关语言和话语模式的方法框架，旨在检测微妙偏见表达而非依赖预定义词汇列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过预定义的词汇或表达列表来诊断偏见，这种方法无法捕捉到微妙的、语境化的偏见表达。需要一种能够处理上下文数据而非孤立词汇或句子的方法，以更准确地表征不同社会群体之间的语言差异。

Method: 使用受控的情境场景和群体标记组合生成上下文数据，创建仅在被引用群体上不同但叙事条件可比的文本最小对。通过语言形式泛化，使用改进的点互信息变体量化语言抽象与群体之间的关联，检测跨群体不成比例出现的表达。采用片段排序策略优先处理具有高浓度偏见语言信号的文本片段。

Result: 该方法能够生成可对比的上下文文本数据，识别出跨群体不成比例出现的语言表达，并通过片段排序帮助专家在语境中评估语言表达的有害潜力，实现定量分析与定性解释的桥梁。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种系统化的方法来发现与不同社会群体相关的微妙语言模式，超越了传统的基于词汇列表的偏见检测方法，能够处理各种文本类型，为偏见研究提供了更全面和语境化的分析工具。

Abstract: We present a methodological framework to discover linguistic and discursive patterns associated to different social groups through contrastive synthetic text generation and statistical analysis. In contrast with previous approaches, we aim to characterize subtle expressions of bias, instead of diagnosing bias through a pre-determined list of words or expressions. We are also working with contextualized data instead of isolated words or sentences. Our methodology applies to textual productions in any genre, encompassing narrative, task-oriented or dialogic. Contextualized data are generated using controlled combinations of situational scenarios and group markers, creating minimal pairs of texts that differ only in the referenced group while maintaining comparable narrative conditions. To facilitate robust analysis, linguistic forms are generalized and associations between linguistic abstractions and groups are quantified using a variant of pointwise mutual information to detect expressions that appear disproportionately across groups. A fragment-ranking strategy then prioritizes text segments with a high concentration of biased linguistic signals, which allows for experts to assess the harmful potential of linguistic expressions in context, bridging quantitative analysis and qualitative interpretation.

</details>


### [166] [DuConTE: Dual-Granularity Text Encoder with Topology-Constrained Attention for Text-attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17411)
*Lexuan Liang,Tao Zou,Xuxiang Ta,Zekun Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DuConTE：一种双粒度文本编码器，通过拓扑约束注意力机制同时处理词级和节点级语义，并整合图结构信息，在文本属性图上实现最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在文本编码阶段仅关注词级语义交互，忽略了不同节点文本之间的结构依赖关系，导致无法充分利用图拓扑信息来增强语义理解。

Method: 提出双粒度文本编码器DuConTE，采用两个预训练语言模型的级联架构：先进行词级粒度编码，再进行节点级粒度编码。在自注意力计算中，根据节点连接性动态调整注意力掩码矩阵，使模型学习图结构指导的语义关联。在从词标记嵌入组合节点表示时，分别评估中心节点上下文和邻居上下文中标记的重要性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，DuConTE在大多数数据集上实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过同时考虑词级和节点级语义交互，并整合图拓扑约束，DuConTE能够更有效地捕捉文本属性图中的语义信息，显著提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs integrate semantic information of node texts with topological structure, offering significant value in various applications such as document classification and information extraction. Existing approaches typically encode textual content using language models (LMs), followed by graph neural networks (GNNs) to process structural information. However, during the LM-based text encoding phase, most methods not only perform semantic interaction solely at the word-token granularity, but also neglect the structural dependencies among texts from different nodes. In this work, we propose DuConTE, a dual-granularity text encoder with topology-constrained attention. The model employs a cascaded architecture of two pretrained LMs, encoding semantics first at the word-token granularity and then at the node granularity. During the self-attention computation in each LM, we dynamically adjust the attention mask matrix based on node connectivity, guiding the model to learn semantic correlations informed by the graph structure. Furthermore, when composing node representations from word-token embeddings, we separately evaluate the importance of tokens under the center-node context and the neighborhood context, enabling the capture of more contextually relevant semantic information. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that DuConTE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of them.

</details>


### [167] [Jupiter-N Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17429)
*George Drayson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jupiter-N是基于Nemotron 3 Super的1200亿参数开源大语言模型的混合推理模型，通过后训练实现了三个目标：基于不确定性的智能体能力、英国文化对齐和威尔士语支持，同时保持基础模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个可复制的后训练模板，为任何国家创建具有文化对齐和语言支持的自主AI模型，同时保持基础模型的推理能力。

Method: 使用Forget-Me-Not框架混合策略内合成回放和策略外任务数据来减轻灾难性遗忘，包含推理和非推理轨迹来保持混合推理能力，通过不确定性筛选轨迹、基于文化规范的合成数据以及平行语料库和LLM翻译的威尔士语对话进行训练。

Result: Jupiter-N在威尔士语评估中显著提升（ARC-Easy +18，MMLU-Lite +5.25），终端使用（Terminal Bench 2 +9.1）和指令跟随（IFBench +4.4）方面表现优异，同时保持了基础模型能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复制的自主后训练模板，通过替换文化知识、机构语料库和目标语言，可以为任何国家创建等效的AI模型管道，所有模型权重和训练数据集均已开源发布。

Abstract: We present Jupiter-N, a hybrid reasoning model post-trained from Nemotron 3 Super, a fully open-source 120 billion parameter LLM. We target three objectives: (1) agentic capability via uncertainty-curated trajectories; (2) UK cultural alignment via synthetic data grounded in cultural norms; and (3) Welsh language support via parallel corpora and LLM-translated Welsh conversations. Our data curation strategy carefully preserves the base model's capabilities: using our Forget-Me-Not framework, we mix on-policy synthetic replay with off-policy task data to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, and include a mixture of reasoning and non-reasoning traces to maintain Nemotron's hybrid reasoning ability. Jupiter-N achieves standout gains over Nemotron in Welsh (+18 on ARC-Easy, +5.25 on MMLU-Lite), terminal-use (+9.1 on Terminal Bench 2) and instruction following (+4.4 on IFBench), while retaining the base model capabilities. We frame this work as a reproducible template for sovereign post-training: substituting cultural knowledge, institutional corpora, and target languages produces an equivalent pipeline for any country. All model weights and all post-training datasets are publicly released under open licences.

</details>


### [168] [Self-Consistency from Only Two Samples: CoT-PoT Ensembling for Efficient LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17433)
*Raman Saparkhan,Majd Hawasly,Md Rizwan Parvez,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoT-PoT混合集成方法，在保持自洽性技术准确性的同时，将所需样本数减少9.3倍，78.6%的任务仅需2个样本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽性技术虽然能提高大语言模型的推理准确性，但需要大量采样导致计算成本高昂。现有方法无法在少量样本下实现有效改进。

Method: 提出混合集成框架，结合思维链和程序思维两种推理模式的优势。设计了完整的采样策略和早期停止策略，通过互补推理路径提高效率。

Result: CoT-PoT集成不仅提高了整体准确性，还将自洽性所需的样本数减少了9.3倍。78.6%的任务仅需2个样本即可解决，这是先前自洽性方法无法实现的。

Conclusion: 混合集成方法通过结合不同推理模式的互补优势，在显著降低计算成本的同时提高了推理准确性，为高效的自洽性技术提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-consistency (SC) is a popular technique for improving the reasoning accuracy of large language models by aggregating multiple sampled outputs, but it comes at a high computational cost due to extensive sampling. We introduce a hybrid ensembling approach that leverages the complementary strengths of two distinct modes of reasoning: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and Program-of-Thought (PoT). We describe a general framework for combining these two forms of reasoning in self-consistency, as well as particular strategies for both full sampling and early-stopping. We show that CoT-PoT ensembling not only improves overall accuracy, but also drastically reduces the number of samples required for SC by a factor of 9.3x. In particular, the majority of tasks (78.6%) can be addressed with only two samples, which has not been possible with any prior SC methods.

</details>


### [169] [MoVE: Translating Laughter and Tears via Mixture of Vocalization Experts in Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17435)
*Szu-Chi Chen,I-Ning Tsai,Yi-Cheng Lin,Sung-Feng Huang,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MoVE架构解决语音翻译中非语言发声丢失问题，通过混合专家模型和少量数据实现高质量表达性语音翻译


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音翻译系统虽然语义准确，但会丢失笑声、哭声等传达语用意图的非语言发声，限制了实际应用价值

Method: 1) 提出可扩展的表达性数据集合成流程；2) 设计MoVE架构，使用表达性专用适配器和软权重路由器混合专家；3) 利用预训练AudioLLM实现数据高效性

Result: 在英中语音翻译中，MoVE在76%的情况下能重现目标非语言发声，获得最高的人类评分自然度和情感保真度，而现有系统最多只能保留14%的非语言发声

Conclusion: MoVE架构通过混合专家模型和少量数据训练，能有效解决语音翻译中非语言发声丢失问题，显著提升表达性和实用性

Abstract: Recent Speech-to-Speech Translation (S2ST) systems achieve strong semantic accuracy yet consistently strip away non-verbal vocalizations (NVs), such as laughter and crying that convey pragmatic intent, which severely limits real-world utility. We address this via three contributions. First, we propose a synthesis pipeline for building scalable expressive datasets to overcome the data scarcity limitation. Second, we propose MoVE, a Mixture-of-LoRA-Experts architecture with expressive-specialized adapters and a soft-weighting router that blends experts for capturing hybrid expressive states. Third, we show pretrained AudioLLMs enable striking data efficiency: 30 minutes of curated data is enough for strong performance. On English-Chinese S2ST, while comparing with strong baselines, MoVE reproduces target NVs in 76% of cases and achieves the highest human-rated naturalness and emotional fidelity among all compared systems, where existing S2ST systems preserve at most 14% of NVs.

</details>


### [170] [OPSDL: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17535)
*Xinsen Zhang,Zhenkai Ding,Tianjun Pan,Run Yang,Chun Kang,Xue Xiong,Jingnan Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OPSDL是一种通过策略内自蒸馏方法增强大语言模型长上下文能力的训练技术，利用模型自身强大的短上下文能力作为"自教师"来监督其在长上下文场景中的生成，无需外部高质量监督数据或稀疏奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展大语言模型的有效上下文长度是实际应用中的核心挑战。现有的后训练方法要么依赖高质量监督数据，要么使用稀疏的序列级奖励，导致优化不稳定且效率低下。需要一种更稳定高效的训练方法来提升模型的长上下文能力。

Method: OPSDL（策略内自蒸馏方法）利用模型自身固有的强大短上下文能力作为"自教师"。模型首先在完整长上下文条件下生成响应，然后自教师通过从相关提取的短上下文计算点对点反向KL散度，提供密集的token级监督信号。这种方法鼓励模型忠实使用相关证据，并减轻由无关上下文引起的幻觉。

Result: 在7B到32B参数范围的多个模型上进行长上下文基准测试，结果显示OPSDL在不同上下文长度下都取得了持续且显著的改进，优于标准的后训练方法如SFT和DPO，且样本效率更高。重要的是，这些改进不会降低模型的一般短上下文性能。

Conclusion: OPSDL作为一种可扩展且稳定的长上下文学习方法，通过利用模型自身短上下文能力作为监督信号，无需外部高质量数据，实现了对长上下文能力的有效提升，同时保持了短上下文性能。

Abstract: Extending the effective context length of large language models (LLMs) remains a central challenge for real-world applications. While recent post-training methods have made progress in long-context scaling, they either rely on high-quality supervision data or sparse sequence-level rewards, leading to unstable and inefficient optimization. We propose OPSDL, an On-Policy Self-Distillation method for enhancing the Long-context capabilities of LLMs. Unlike other recent self-distillation methods that inject privileged information and rely on the model's in-context learning ability to act as a teacher, OPSDL leverages the model's own inherently strong short-context capability as a self-teacher to supervise its own generation in long-context scenarios. The model first generates responses conditioned on the full long-context, then the self-teacher provides per-token supervision signals via point-wise reverse KL divergence under the relevant extracted short-context. This dense token-level signal encourages faithful use of relevant evidence and mitigates hallucinations induced by irrelevant context. We evaluate OPSDL on long-context benchmarks across a range of models from 7B to 32B parameters. Results show consistent and substantial improvements across varying context lengths, outperforming standard post-training approaches such as SFT and DPO with higher sample efficiency. Notably, these gains are achieved without degrading general short-context performance. These findings highlight the effectiveness of OPSDL as a scalable and stable approach for long-context learning.

</details>


### [171] [PoliLegalLM: A Technical Report on a Large Language Model for Political and Legal Affairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17543)
*Yuting Huang,Yinghao Hu,Qian Xiao,Wenlin Zhong,Yiquan Wu,Taishi Zhou,Moke Chen,Changlong Sun,Kun Kuang,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PoliLegalLM是一个专门为政治法律领域设计的大语言模型，通过统一训练框架解决LLM在法律应用中存在的幻觉引用、知识覆盖不全和推理能力弱的问题，在多个法律基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用领域取得了显著成功，但直接应用于法律领域仍面临挑战：1) 产生幻觉的法律引用；2) 知识覆盖不完整；3) 结构化推理能力弱。需要专门针对法律领域的LLM来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用统一训练框架，整合了持续预训练、渐进式监督微调和基于偏好的强化学习。构建了大规模高质量法律语料库，设计了结构化后训练流程，使模型能有效学习领域知识和适应多样化法律任务。

Result: 在三个代表性基准测试（LawBench、LexEval和真实世界数据集PoliLegal）上评估，PoliLegalLM表现出强大且一致的性能，优于相似规模的竞争模型，与显著更大的模型相比仍保持高度竞争力，在真实法律场景中取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 研究证明了训练范式的有效性，以及领域特定LLM在真实世界法律应用中的实用价值。PoliLegalLM为解决法律领域LLM应用的关键挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in general-domain tasks, yet their direct application to the legal domain remains challenging due to hallucinated legal citations, incomplete knowledge coverage, and weak structured reasoning. To address these issues, we propose PoliLegalLM, a domain-specific large language model tailored for political and legal applications. Our approach adopts a unified training framework that integrates continued pretraining, progressive supervised fine-tuning, and preference-based reinforcement learning to jointly enhance legal knowledge grounding, task alignment, and reasoning capability. We construct a large-scale, high-quality legal corpus and design a structured post-training pipeline, enabling the model to effectively learn domain-specific knowledge and adapt to diverse legal tasks. We evaluate PoliLegalLM on three representative benchmarks, including LawBench, LexEval, and a real-world dataset, PoliLegal. Experimental results demonstrate that PoliLegalLM achieves strong and consistent performance, outperforming competitive models of similar scale and remaining highly competitive with significantly larger models, while achieving the best results on real-world legal scenarios. These results highlight the effectiveness of our training paradigm and the practical value of domain-specific LLMs for real-world legal applications.

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Fine-Tuning: In-Context Learning and Chain-of-Thought for Reasoned Distractor Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17574)
*Elaf Alhazmi,Quan Z. Sheng,Wei Emma Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLMs）和上下文学习的干扰项生成框架，通过无监督语义检索选择少样本示例，联合生成干扰项及其推理依据，在多个基准测试中取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干扰项生成目前仍高度依赖领域专家，现有方法虽然通过微调预训练模型和对比学习生成语义相关的干扰项，但往往无法捕捉专家在选择干扰项时的底层推理过程。

Method: 提出基于大语言模型的推理增强干扰项生成框架：1）使用无监督语义检索选择少样本示例；2）通过上下文学习让LLMs生成干扰项；3）设计联合生成框架，同时生成干扰项及其推理依据。

Result: 在六个基准测试（涵盖不同干扰项长度和领域）上的实验表明，使用少样本示例提示LLMs的方法显著优于现有DG模型，在生成符合人类标注基准的推理干扰项方面达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 大语言模型通过上下文学习和推理增强框架能够有效生成高质量的干扰项，该方法减少了专家依赖，在干扰项生成任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Distractor generation (DG) remains a labor-intensive task that still significantly depends on domain experts. The task focuses on generating plausible yet incorrect options, known as distractors, for multiple-choice questions. A reliable distractor must be contextually relevant to the question and able to mislead examinees through implicit reasoning when identifying the correct answer. While a recent method integrates fine-tuning pre-trained encoder-decoder models with contrastive learning to generate semantically relevant distractors for a given question-answer, it often fails to capture the underlying reasoning process that experts utilize when selecting distractors in benchmarks. In this paper, we explore large language models (LLMs) reasoning for DG through in-context learning with unsupervised semantic retrieval for selecting few-shot examples. We design a rationale-augmented DG framework that jointly generates distractors and their rationales for a given question-answer. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks, with varying average distractor lengths and domains, demonstrate that prompting LLMs with few-shot examples substantially improves the performance compared to recent DG models. It outperforms recent approaches and achieves state-of-the-art results in generating reasoned distractors that align with human-labeled benchmarks.

</details>


### [173] [Agents Explore but Agents Ignore: LLMs Lack Environmental Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17609)
*Leon Engländer,Sophia Althammer,Ahmet Üstün,Matthias Gallé,Tom Sherborne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前基于LLM的智能体缺乏环境好奇心，即使意外发现完整任务解决方案，也很少利用这些发现来调整策略或最大化利用有用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于LLM的智能体是否能够有效整合环境观察信息，特别是当意外发现高度相关但意想不到的信息时，能否利用这些发现来改进推理和决策。

Method: 在三个基准测试（Terminal-Bench、SWE-Bench、AppWorld）中注入完整任务解决方案，故意向模型暴露任务解法。分析智能体发现和利用这些解决方案的情况，识别影响环境好奇心的主要因素：可用工具、测试时计算资源和训练数据分布。

Result: 智能体在Terminal-Bench中发现解决方案的比例为79-81%，但只有37-50%的案例会利用这些发现。在AppWorld中，超过90%的尝试中智能体能看到"返回此任务完整解决方案"的文档，但只有不到7%的试验会利用这一信息。优化配置以最大化好奇心的智能体在未修改基准测试中表现最佳，但即使在优化后，大多数试验中智能体仍会忽略发现的解决方案。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的智能体缺乏环境好奇心，它们主要使用环境来获取预期信息，而不是修订策略或最大化利用有用刺激。智能体配置、计算资源和训练数据分布是影响环境好奇心的关键因素。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are assumed to integrate environmental observations into their reasoning: discovering highly relevant but unexpected information should naturally lead to a model exploiting its own discoveries. We show that this assumption is false for current LLM-based agents, which struggle to reflect or react to unexpected information. Across three benchmarks (Terminal-Bench, SWE-Bench, AppWorld), we inject complete task solutions into the agent environments to deliberately expose a task's solution to a model. While agents discover these solutions on Terminal-Bench in 79-81% of runs, they interact, or exploit, them in only 37-50% of cases. This gap is starkest in AppWorld: agents see documentation stating that a command "returns the complete solution to this task" in over 90% of attempts but exploit this in fewer than 7% of trials. We show that agents lack what we call environmental curiosity: the capability to recognize and investigate unexpected but relevant observations in response to environmental stimuli. We identify three main factors influencing environmental curiosity: available tools in the agent scaffold, test-time compute, and training data distribution. Our findings identify configurations that maximize curiosity also achieve the best performance on the unmodified benchmarks. Yet even jointly optimized agents still ignore discovered solutions in the majority of trials: current agents use the environment to fetch expected information, but not to revise their strategy or maximally exploit useful stimuli.

</details>


### [174] [Does Welsh media need a review? Detecting bias in Nation.Cymru's political reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17628)
*Cai Parry-Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用NLP技术分析威尔士媒体Nation.Cymru的政治偏见，发现改革党受到负面偏见报道的频率是威尔士党的两倍，且负面情绪强度高三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对威尔士媒体存在政治偏见指控日益增长的现象，首次采用计算方法验证这些说法，为更广泛的威尔士媒体审查提供证据基础。

Method: 采用两阶段自然语言处理流程：1) 使用RoBERTa偏见检测器进行高效偏见发现；2) 使用大语言模型对检测到的偏见标签进行目标属性情感分类。

Result: 主要分析显示改革党在2022-2026年新闻文章中受到偏见框架的频率是威尔士党的两倍，平均负面情绪强度高三倍以上(p<0.001)。次要分析表明威尔士党是异常值，在所有党派中获得明显更有利的框架。

Conclusion: 研究提供了威尔士单一政治媒体存在可测量的差异框架的证据，支持对威尔士媒体报道进行更广泛审查的呼吁。两阶段流程为扩展到其他威尔士媒体乃至威尔士以外的媒体生态系统提供了低成本、可复制的框架。

Abstract: Wales' political landscape has been marked by growing accusations of bias in Welsh media. This paper takes the first computational step toward testing those claims by examining Nation.Cymru, a prominent Welsh political news outlet. I use a two-stage natural language processing (NLP) pipeline: (1) a robustly optimized BERT approach (RoBERTa) bias detector for efficient bias discovery and (2) a large language model (LLM) for target-attributed sentiment classification of bias labels from (1). A primary analysis of 15,583 party mentions across 2022-2026 news articles finds that Reform UK attracts biased framing at twice the rate of Plaid Cymru and over three times as negative in mean sentiment (p<0.001). A secondary analysis across four parties across both news and opinion articles shows that Plaid Cymru is the outlier, receiving markedly more favourable framing than any other party. These findings provide evidence of measurable differential framing in a single Welsh political media outlet, supporting calls for a broader review of Welsh media coverage. Furthermore, the two-stage pipeline offers a low-cost, replicable framework for extending this analysis to other Welsh outlets, as well as media ecosystems outside of Wales.

</details>


### [175] [Copy First, Translate Later: Interpreting Translation Dynamics in Multilingual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17633)
*Felicia Körner,Maria Matveev,Florian Eichin,Gitta Kutyniok,Barbara Plank,Michael A. Hedderich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过细粒度追踪多语言模型训练早期阶段，揭示了跨语言泛化能力的形成过程，发现翻译能力发展经历两个阶段：初始的复制和表层相似性阶段，以及后续的泛化翻译机制发展阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常通过孤立因素和稀疏的训练检查点来分析多语言模型的跨语言能力，这限制了对跨语言泛化能力如何形成（特别是在训练早期阶段）的理解。

Method: 在9种不同语言上预训练一个17亿参数的多语言模型，以更细粒度捕获检查点；引入新的词级翻译数据集；通过行为分析、模型组件分析和参数消融实验追踪翻译能力在训练过程中的发展。

Result: 模型快速获得基本语言能力的同时进行词元级复制；翻译能力发展分为两个阶段：第一阶段以复制和表层相似性为主，第二阶段发展出更泛化的翻译机制同时优化复制能力。

Conclusion: 这些发现提供了多语言预训练期间跨语言泛化能力如何发展的细粒度视角，揭示了翻译能力从表层复制到深层泛化的演变过程。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit impressive cross-lingual capabilities. However, prior work analyzes this phenomenon through isolated factors and at sparse points during training, limiting our understanding of how cross-lingual generalization emerges--particularly in the early phases of learning. To study the early trajectory of linguistic and translation capabilities, we pretrain a multilingual 1.7B model on nine diverse languages, capturing checkpoints at a much finer granularity. We further introduce a novel word-level translation dataset and trace how translation develops over training through behavioral analyses, model-component analysis, and parameter-based ablations. We find that the model quickly acquires basic linguistic capabilities in parallel with token-level copying, while translation develops in two distinct phases: an initial phase dominated by copying and surface-level similarities, and a second phase in which more generalizing translation mechanisms are developed while copying is refined. Together, these findings provide a fine-grained view of how cross-lingual generalization develops during multilingual pretraining.

</details>


### [176] [ThreadSumm: Summarization of Nested Discourse Threads Using Tree of Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17648)
*Olubusayo Olabisi,Ekata Mitra,Ameeta Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThreadSumm：一个多阶段LLM框架，通过分层推理处理深度嵌套讨论线程的摘要生成，相比现有基线在逻辑结构和观点覆盖方面表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM摘要生成器在处理深度嵌套讨论线程时存在困难，难以可靠捕捉交错的回复、引用和重叠主题，需要更有效的方法来生成结构化的线程摘要。

Method: ThreadSumm采用多阶段框架：1) 通过LLM提取话语方面和原子内容单元进行内容规划；2) 应用句子排序构建线程感知序列，展现多个观点而非单一线性线索；3) 在可解释单元上使用思维树搜索，生成并评分多个段落候选，在统一搜索空间中联合优化连贯性和覆盖度。

Result: ThreadSumm在生成逻辑结构摘要方面相比现有基线表现更好，同时在嵌套讨论中实现了更高的话语方面保留和观点覆盖度。

Conclusion: ThreadSumm通过将线程摘要视为分层推理问题，采用多提案和迭代优化设计，能够更有效地处理深度嵌套讨论线程，生成结构更好、覆盖更全面的摘要。

Abstract: Summarizing deeply nested discussion threads requires handling interleaved replies, quotes, and overlapping topics, which standard LLM summarizers struggle to capture reliably. We introduce ThreadSumm, a multi-stage LLM framework that treats thread summarization as a hierarchical reasoning problem over explicit aspect and content unit representations. Our method first performs content planning via LLM-based extraction of discourse aspects and Atomic Content Units, then applies sentence ordering to construct thread-aware sequences that surface multiple viewpoints rather than a single linear strand. On top of these interpretable units, ThreadSumm employs a Tree of Thoughts search that generates and scores multiple paragraph candidates, jointly optimizing coherence and coverage within a unified search space. With this multi-proposal and iterative refinement design, we show improved performance in generating logically structured summaries compared to existing baselines, while achieving higher aspect retention and opinion coverage in nested discussions.

</details>


### [177] [Measuring Distribution Shift in User Prompts and Its Effects on LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17650)
*Parker Seegmiller,Sarah Masud Preum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LENS框架，用于量化自然提示分布偏移及其对部署LLMs性能的影响，发现即使是中等程度的用户提示行为偏移也会导致LLM性能大幅下降（平均损失73%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在动态现实环境中部署时，用户提示分布会随时间、任务和用户群体变化而显著偏移，这种自然提示分布偏移对LLM可靠性构成重大挑战，特别是针对特定领域或用户群体的专用模型。目前缺乏对自然提示分布偏移的量化测量研究，其对部署LLMs的影响也知之甚少。

Method: 提出LLM评估自然提示偏移（LENS）框架，这是一种数据驱动的方法，用于量化自然提示分布偏移并评估其对部署LLMs性能的影响。研究进行了大规模评估，涵盖192个真实世界部署后提示偏移场景（时间、用户群体、地理三个维度），训练了81个模型（基于468万训练提示），并在5.76万个提示上进行评估。

Result: 研究发现：1）即使是中等程度的用户提示行为偏移也会导致部署LLMs性能大幅下降（平均损失73%）；2）性能下降在来自不同潜在群体和地理区域的用户与模型交互时尤为普遍；3）性能下降与时间上的自然提示分布偏移相关；4）系统性地描述了LLM指令跟随能力如何随时间推移和不同用户群体间退化。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了数据驱动监控的迫切需求，以确保LLM性能在不同且不断演变的用户群体中保持稳定。自然提示分布偏移是LLM部署可靠性的重要挑战，需要系统性的评估和监控方法。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in dynamic, real-world settings, where the distribution of user prompts can shift substantially over time as new tasks, prompts, and users are introduced to a deployed model. Such natural prompt distribution shift poses a major challenge to LLM reliability, particularly for specialized models designed for narrow domains or user populations. Despite attention to out-of-distribution robustness, there is very limited exploration of measuring natural prompt distribution shift in prior work, and its impact on deployed LLMs remains poorly understood. We introduce the LLM Evaluation under Natural prompt Shift (LENS) framework: a data-centric approach for quantifying natural prompt distribution shift and evaluating its effect on the performance of deployed LLMs. We perform a large-scale evaluation using 192 real-world post-deployment prompt shift settings over time, user group, and geographic axes, training a total of 81 models on 4.68M training prompts, and evaluating on 57.6k prompts. We find that even moderate shifts in user prompt behavior correspond with large performance drops (73% average loss) in deployed LLMs. This performance degradation is particularly prevalent when users from different latent groups and geographic regions interact with models and is correlated with natural prompt distribution shift over time. We systematically characterize how LLM instruction following ability degrades over time and between user groups. Our findings highlight the critical need for data-driven monitoring to ensure LLM performance remains stable across diverse and evolving user populations.

</details>


### [178] [Semantic Density Effect (SDE): Maximizing Information Per Token Improves LLM Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17659)
*Amr Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出语义密度效应（SDE）：提示中每个token携带的语义信息越高，LLM输出越准确、专注且幻觉越少。通过提升语义密度（减少低信息token）而非增加token数，可在零额外成本下显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化技术（如思维链、提示重复、指令放置效应）通常通过增加token数量或调整结构来提升性能，但会增加计算成本和延迟。作者发现语义密度这一关键因素被忽视，高语义密度的提示能更有效地引导模型生成优质输出。

Method: 定义语义密度效应（SDE）为语义负载token与总提示token的比率，并调整冗余性和具体性。通过移除或替换低信息token来提升语义密度，同时保持或增强语义信号。在5个前沿模型和7个基准测试上进行评估。

Result: 超高密度提示（SDE > 0.80）相比稀释版本平均提升8.4个百分点，且无需额外token和延迟开销。与指令放置效应（IPE）结合使用时，提升可达11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 语义密度是提示工程中被忽视的关键因素，通过优化语义密度而非增加token数量，可以在零成本下显著提升LLM性能。SDE为高效提示优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Semantic Density Effect (SDE): the empirical finding that prompts carrying higher semantic information per token consistently produce more accurate, focused, and less hallucinated outputs across all major LLM families. SDE is defined as the ratio of semantically loaded tokens to total prompt tokens, adjusted for redundancy and concreteness. Unlike prior prompt optimization techniques that add tokens (Chain of Thought), duplicate the prompt (Prompt Repetition), or reorder components (Instruction Placement Effect), SDE improves performance by removing or replacing low-information tokens while preserving or sharpening the semantic signal. Evaluated across five frontier models and seven benchmarks, ultra-dense prompts (SDE > 0.80) outperform diluted counterparts by an average of +8.4 percentage points with 0 additional tokens and 0 latency overhead. Combined with Instruction Placement Effect (IPE), the gain reaches +11.7 percentage points

</details>


### [179] [Peerispect: Claim Verification in Scientific Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17667)
*Ali Ghorbanpour,Soroush Sadeghian,Alireza Daghighfarsoodeh,Sajad Ebrahimi,Negar Arabzadeh,Seyed Mohammad Hosseini,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Peerispect是一个交互式系统，通过提取同行评审中的可核查声明、从稿件中检索相关证据，并使用自然语言推理进行验证，实现同行评审的声明级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审是科学出版的核心，但评审意见常包含主观、修辞性或与提交工作不符的声明。在会议和期刊规模庞大的背景下，手动检查这些声明的依据是不可行的，需要自动化的验证工具来确保公平性和问责制。

Method: Peerispect采用模块化信息检索管道，包括：1）从同行评审中提取值得核查的声明；2）从稿件中检索相关证据；3）通过自然语言推理验证声明；4）通过可视化界面展示结果，在论文中高亮显示证据。

Result: 开发了公开可用的演示系统（https://app.reviewer.ly/app/peerispect）和API服务（https://github.com/Reviewerly-Inc/Peerispect），并配有视频教程。系统支持替代的检索器、重排序器和验证器。

Conclusion: Peerispect为评审者、作者和程序委员会提供了一个实用的工具，能够快速检查和解释同行评审声明的依据，提高了评审过程的透明度和可靠性。

Abstract: Peer review is central to scientific publishing, yet reviewers frequently include claims that are subjective, rhetorical, or misaligned with the submitted work. Assessing whether review statements are factual and verifiable is crucial for fairness and accountability. At the scale of modern conferences and journals, manually inspecting the grounding of such claims is infeasible. We present Peerispect, an interactive system that operationalizes claim-level verification in peer reviews by extracting check-worthy claims from peer reviews, retrieving relevant evidence from the manuscript, and verifying the claims through natural language inference. Results are presented through a visual interface that highlights evidence directly in the paper, enabling rapid inspection and interpretation. Peerispect is designed as a modular Information Retrieval (IR) pipeline, supporting alternative retrievers, rerankers, and verifiers, and is intended for use by reviewers, authors, and program committees. We demonstrate Peerispect through a live, publicly available demo (https://app.reviewer.ly/app/peerispect) and API services (https://github.com/Reviewerly-Inc/Peerispect), accompanied by a video tutorial (https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pc9RkvkUh14).

</details>


### [180] [Before You Interpret the Profile: Validity Scaling for LLM Metacognitive Self-Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17707)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将临床人格评估中的效度量表框架应用于大语言模型评估，通过6个效度指标识别模型回答的有效性，发现部分模型存在效度问题，且有效模型能产生项目敏感的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床人格评估在解释实质性量表前会先筛查回答效度，但当前的大语言模型评估缺乏类似的效度检验机制。研究者希望将人格评估中的效度量表框架应用于大语言模型，以评估其回答的有效性。

Method: 研究将人格评估量表（PAI和MMPI-3）中的效度标定框架应用于大语言模型的元认知探针数据，涵盖20个前沿模型的524个项目。操作化了6个效度指标：L（对错误保持信心）、K（押注错误）、F（撤回共识认可项目）、Fp（撤回正确答案）、RBS（反转监控）和TRIN（固定回答）。采用分层分类系统识别模型效度。

Result: 识别出4个模型为结构层面无效，2个为升高水平。有效模型能产生项目敏感的置信度（平均r=0.18，16个中有14个显著），而无效模型则不能（平均r=-0.20，d=2.17，p=0.001）。思维链训练产生两种相反的回答扭曲。两个潜在维度解释了94.6%的指标变异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型评估需要效度筛查机制，类似临床人格评估的做法。研究开发了便携式筛查协议，并验证了其对抗选择性预测的有效性。所有数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Clinical personality assessment screens response validity before interpreting substantive scales. LLM evaluation does not. We apply the validity scaling framework from the PAI and MMPI-3 to metacognitive probe data from 20 frontier models across 524 items. Six validity indices are operationalised: L (maintaining confidence on errors), K (betting on errors), F (withdrawing consensus-endorsed items), Fp (withdrawing correct answers), RBS (inverted monitoring), and TRIN (fixed responding). A tiered classification system identifies four models as construct-level invalid and two as elevated. Valid-profile models produce item-sensitive confidence (mean r = .18, 14 of 16 significant). Invalid-profile models do not (mean r = -.20, d = 2.17, p = .001). Chain-of-thought training produces two opposite response distortions. Two latent dimensions account for 94.6% of index variance. Companion papers extract a portable screening protocol (Cacioli, 2026e) and validate it against selective prediction (Cacioli, 2026f). All data and code: https://github.com/synthiumjp/validity-scaling-llm

</details>


### [181] [DeInfer: Efficient Parallel Inferencing for Decomposed Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17709)
*You-Liang Huang,Xinhao Huang,Chengxi Liao,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeInfer是一个专门用于分解后大语言模型并行推理的高性能推理系统，通过多项优化技术提升性能并兼容现有优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型分解研究主要关注下游任务性能提升，但忽视了模型规模扩大时并行推理性能较差的问题，需要解决这一重要的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出DeInfer系统，包含多项优化技术来最大化性能，同时兼容最先进的优化技术，专门用于分解后大语言模型的并行推理。

Result: 通过大量实验评估DeInfer的性能，结果显示其具有优越性，能够极大地促进分解后大语言模型的并行推理。

Conclusion: DeInfer系统有效解决了分解后大语言模型并行推理的性能问题，为大规模语言模型的推理提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Existing works on large language model (LLM) decomposition mainly focus on improving performance on downstream tasks, but they ignore the poor parallel inference performance when trying to scale up the model size. To mitigate this important performance issue, this paper introduces DeInfer, a high-performance inference system dedicated to parallel inference of decomposed LLMs. It consists of multiple optimizations to maximize performance and be compatible with state-of-the-art optimization techniques. Extensive experiments are carried out to evaluate DeInfer's performance, where the results demonstrate its superiority, suggesting it can greatly facilitate the parallel inference of decomposed LLMs.

</details>


### [182] [Screen Before You Interpret: A Portable Validity Protocol for Benchmark-Based LLM Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17714)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种从临床人格评估移植的协议，用于检查LLM置信度信号是否携带项目级信息，包括三个核心指标、一个结构指标和项目敏感性统计，通过三层次分类系统验证了20个前沿LLM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM置信度信号被用于弃权、路由和安全关键决策，但目前缺乏标准方法来检查置信度信号是否携带项目级信息。作者希望建立一个可移植的基准测试协议来评估LLM置信度数据的有效性

Method: 从临床人格评估（PAI、MMPI-3）移植有效性筛选原则作为可移植协议，包括三个核心指标（L、Fp、RBS）、一个结构指标（TRIN）和基于2x2列联表的项目敏感性统计，使用三层次分类系统（无效、不确定、有效）

Result: 在20个前沿LLM的524个项目上验证，4个模型被分类为无效，2个为不确定。有效配置模型平均r=0.18（15/16显著），无效配置模型平均r=-0.20（d=2.48）。在MMLU和外部数据上的跨基准验证确认了筛选方法的可移植性

Conclusion: 提出的有效性筛选协议能够可靠地区分LLM置信度信号是否携带项目级信息，为LLM置信度评估提供了标准化方法，具有跨基准和探测格式的可移植性

Abstract: LLM confidence signals are used for abstention, routing, and safety-critical decisions. No standard practice exists for checking whether a confidence signal carries item-level information before building on it. We transfer the validity screening principle from clinical personality assessment (PAI, MMPI-3) as a portable protocol for benchmark-based LLM confidence data. The protocol specifies three core indices (L, Fp, RBS), a structural indicator (TRIN), and an item-sensitivity statistic, computed from a single 2x2 contingency table. A three-tier classification system (Invalid, Indeterminate, Valid) draws on four clinical traditions. Validated on 20 frontier LLMs across 524 items, four models are classified Invalid, two Indeterminate. Valid-profile models show mean r = .18 (15/16 significant). Invalid-profile models show mean r = -.20 (d = 2.48). Cross-benchmark validation on 18 models using MMLU with verbalized confidence and on external data from Yang et al. (2024) confirms the screen transfers across benchmarks and probe formats. All data and code: https://github.com/synthiumjp/validity-scaling-llm

</details>


### [183] [Concurrent Criterion Validation of a Validity Screen for LLM Confidence Signals via Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17716)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 有效性筛选将LLM置信度信号分为有效、不确定和无效三类，这些分类能显著预测选择性预测性能，有效模型表现最好，无效模型最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证有效性筛选分类（有效、不确定、无效）是否能预测大型语言模型在选择性预测任务中的性能表现，为模型置信度评估提供实用工具。

Method: 使用有效性筛选对20个前沿LLM的置信度信号进行分类，在524个项目、6个认知轨迹上进行评估，通过Type 2 AUROC指标衡量选择性预测性能，并进行分半交叉验证。

Result: 有效模型的平均AUROC为0.624，无效模型为0.357，效应量d=2.81，p=0.002；三类模型性能呈单调顺序：无效<不确定<有效；该分类解释了AUROC方差的47%。

Conclusion: 有效性筛选能有效预测LLM的选择性预测性能，对于选择性预测任务，置信度信号的分类至关重要，有效模型在选择性预测中表现最佳。

Abstract: The validity screen (Cacioli, 2026d, 2026e) classifies LLM confidence signals as Valid, Indeterminate, or Invalid. We test whether these classifications predict selective prediction performance. Twenty frontier LLMs from seven families were evaluated on 524 items across six cognitive tracks. Valid models show mean Type 2 AUROC = .624 (SD = .048). Invalid models show mean AUROC = .357 (SD = .231). Cohen's d = 2.81, p = .002. The tiers order monotonically: Invalid (.357) < Indeterminate (.554) < Valid (.624). Split-half cross-validation yields median d = 1.77, P(d > 0) = 1.0 across 1,000 splits. The three-tier classification accounts for 47% of the variance in AUROC. DeepSeek-R1 drops from 85.3% accuracy at full coverage to 11.3% at 10% coverage. The screen predicts the criterion. For selective prediction, the screen matters.

</details>
